### [97] [Reinforcement Learning based DBMS Buffer Pool Auto-Tuning for Optimal Memory Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11239)
*Yifan Wang,Patrick Royer,Raphaël Féraud,David Delande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroTune，一个基于在线强化学习的数据库缓冲调节系统，在保证SLA合规的前提下最小化不必要的内存分配，实验证明其在动态工作负载下显著节省内存并减少SLA违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DBA在管理数据库管理系统（DBMS）实例时需要在SLA性能与资源使用之间取得平衡，常常过度分配RAM导致内存浪费，亟需自动化的缓冲调节方案。

Method: 采用在线强化学习（RL）的缓冲调整系统，评估多种RL算法，并利用外部指标（延迟、吞吐量）和内部DBMS指标（状态变量、性能统计）的大量追踪数据进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明MicroTune能动态适应工作负载波动，在节省大量内存的同时比基线方法产生更少的SLA违规。

Conclusion: 研究证明了强化学习在DBMS环境中用于自适应资源管理的潜力。

Abstract: Administering Database Management Systems (DBMS) instances requires Database Administrators (DBA) to balance performance in terms of Service Level Agreement (SLA) against resource usage, often prompting RAM over-allocation that wastes memory. We introduce MicroTune, an online RL-based buffer adjustment system that minimizes unnecessary memory allocation while ensuring SLA compliance. To identify the most effective RL core, we evaluate multiple algorithms under diverse benchmark workloads, training MicroTune on extensive traces of both external metrics (latency, throughput) and internal DBMS metrics (status variables and performance statistics). Experimental results demonstrate that MicroTune dynamically adapts buffer sizes to workload fluctuations, outperforming baselines by achieving significant memory savings with fewer SLA violations. These findings underscore the promise of reinforcement learning for adaptive resource management in DBMS environments.

</details>


### [98] [Reverse Migration of Cloud Applications to On-premises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11640)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Diel（Tursio本地迁移器），一个自动化将云应用反向迁移到本地环境的工具，通过模拟、复制和委派三种策略将云服务转换为本地对应服务，使Tursio AI的云版本和本地版本保持同步，每三周发布一次新稳定版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管行业出于安全和合规原因仍偏好本地部署，但供应商需要在云端开发，导致开发和部署环境不一致，产生漫长的发布周期和复杂的维护负担。

Method: Diel采用模拟（simulate）、复制（replicate）和委派（delegate）三种策略的组合，自动化地将云应用反向迁移到本地环境，将云服务转换为本地对应的服务。

Result: 使用Diel后，Tursio AI能够保持云端和本地版本的同步，每三周发布新的稳定版本。

Conclusion: Diel在实践中成功解决了云环境开发与本地环境部署之间的矛盾，显著缩短了发布周期并简化了维护过程，为类似需要反向迁移的企业提供了可行方案和经验教训。

Abstract: Cloud has become ubiquitous to modern applications due to its agility and scalability. However, regulated industries still prefer to deploy on-premises due to security and compliance reasons. This creates a paradox for vendors who need to develop in the cloud but deploy on-premises, leading to long release cycles and complex maintenance. In this paper, we present Diel, the Tursio On-premises Migrator, a tool that automates reverse migration of cloud applications to on-premises environments. Diel applies a combination of simulate, replicate, and delegate strategies to transform cloud services into on-premises counterparts.We describe the design and implementation of Diel, along with lessons learned from using it in practice. With Diel, we have been able to keep Tursio AI's cloud and on-premises versions in sync, releasing new stable versions every three weeks.

</details>


### [99] [Guided Table Retrieval for Structured Data Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11644)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四阶段"引导式表检索"流水线，结合确定性锚定、连接图结构探索、LLM语义消歧和算法合并，用于在结构化数据库上进行自然语言问答时的相关表和连接树识别。在BIRD-DEV和BEAVER基准上分别达到94%和70%的精确率、92%和53%的F1分数，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言问答到结构化数据库查询的核心挑战是识别相关数据表并确定表间连接关系，这需要同时具备数据库模式知识和对用户意图的语义理解。端到端的LLM方法在处理此类任务时过于脆弱，容易出错，因此需要一种更稳健的分解式方法。

Method: 提出四阶段流水线：(1) 基于哈希预测器的确定性锚定，确保基础覆盖；(2) 通过连接图可达性进行结构探索以扩大候选范围；(3) 利用LLM对源表和目标表进行语义消歧；(4) 通过算法将结果合并为最小且拓扑有序的连接树。每个阶段承担独立职责（确定性、覆盖性、语义推理、连贯性），避免端到端LLM方法的脆弱性。

Result: 在BIRD-DEV基准上达到94%精确率和92% F1，在企业级BEAVER基准上达到70%精确率和53% F1，在精确率和F1指标上均大幅超越现有基线，且生成的连接树可直接供下游查询编译器使用。

Conclusion: 通过将表检索任务分解为确定性、覆盖性、语义推理和连贯性四个异质阶段，可以克服端到端LLM方法的脆弱性，在利用LLM上下文判断能力的同时确保输出的精确性和可用性，生成的精确连接树可直接供下游查询编译器消费。

Abstract: Answering natural language questions over structured databases requires identifying the relevant tables and determining how to join them---a task that demands both schema knowledge and semantic understanding of the user's intent.We present guided table retrieval, a four-phase pipeline that combines deterministic grounding via hash-based predictors, structural exploration of join-graph reachability, LLM-powered disambiguation of sources and targets, and algorithmic merging into minimal, topologically ordered join trees. By decomposing the problem into phases with distinct responsibilities--- determinism, coverage, semantic reasoning, and coherence--- the pipeline avoids the brittleness of end-to-end LLM approaches while leveraging LLMs where their contextual judgment is most needed. We evaluate on BIRD-DEV and the enterprise-scale BEAVER benchmark, achieving 94% and 70% precision respectively, with 92% and 53% F1---substantially outperforming existing baselines on precision and F1 while producing exact join trees that can be directly consumed by downstream query compilers.

</details>


### [100] [Enabling Differentiated QoS Degradation for Replicated Databases under Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11836)
*Belkis Djeffal,Pierre Bourhis,Romain Rouvoy*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对数据库复制服务在失效场景下的差异化QoS降级问题，提出了一种修复到目标（repair-to-target）策略，并通过PostgreSQL JDBC中间件负载均衡器PLB实现服务类别感知的路由。PLB在故障后更新健康副本的角色分配（Premium、Mixed、Freemium），在共享副本池中保持各类别的差异化服务水平。实验表明，PLB在Premium端故障时中位Premium吞吐保持率提升26-28个百分点，在最严重级联故障阶段Premium吞吐提升2倍以上，p95延迟降低18.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上弹性（elasticity）被视为容量损失后的默认恢复手段，但副本替换需要时间同步和额外资源投入，在预算固定或运行受限时不可行。当服务暴露差异化服务等级时，容量损失不能统一处理，需要在容量降低的情况下明确控制每类服务的降级程度，同时保留原有的差异化特性。因此需要一种明确的降级控制机制而非简单的容量恢复。

Method: 提出repair-to-target策略，实现在PLB（PostgreSQL JDBC中间件负载均衡器）中。故障发生后，PLB将健康副本动态分配为Premium、Mixed、Freemium三种角色，保持副本池共享的同时确保新会话分配仍然反映服务类别。在单副本崩溃和级联崩溃两种场景下，在隔离的每类副本池和共享的优先级无关路由两种部署策略中进行评估。

Result: PLB在Premium端故障下使中位Premium吞吐保持率提升26-28个百分点；在最严重的级联故障阶段，Premium吞吐提升超过2倍；相比共享轮询（round-robin），Premium的p95延迟降低18.2%。

Conclusion: 差异化QoS降级应被视为服务行为的组成部分，而非简单依赖弹性容量恢复。通过类别感知的角色分配和路由控制，可以在容量受限时保留各类服务的差异化水平，为复制数据库服务提供更符合实际运行约束的失效处理方式。

Abstract: Elasticity is commonly presented as the default response to capacity loss after failures, since replacement replicas can compensate for failed nodes and restore pre-incident service levels. Replacement capacity entails both delay and additional resource commitment, as replicas must be provisioned and synchronized before they can serve traffic. Under fixed budgets or constrained operating conditions, capacity restoration cannot be treated as the immediate recovery path. Failure handling must instead define how the service continues while capacity remains reduced. When the service exposes differentiated service levels, capacity loss cannot be handled uniformly. Degradation becomes part of the service behavior, requiring explicit control over how reduced capacity affects each class without erasing the intended differentiation.  We study differentiated QoS degradation in replicated database services with service-class-aware sessions. We present a repair-to-target policy, implemented in PLB, a PostgreSQL JDBC middleware load balancer for service-class-aware routing. When fail-stop failures remove part of the available capacity, PLB updates the role assignment of healthy replicas into Premium, Mixed, and Freemium roles. This keeps the replica pool shared while ensuring that new session assignments continue to reflect the service class. We evaluate PLB under single and cascading replica failures across two deployment strategies: isolated perclass replica pools and shared, priority-agnostic routing. The results show that PLB improves median Premium goodput retention by 26-28 percentage points under a Premium-side fault, achieves more than 2x higher Premium goodput in the most severe cascading-failure phase, and reduces Premium p95 latency by 18.2% relative to shared round-robin.

</details>


### [101] [DexterSQL: Deep Schema Exploration and Rule-based Correction for Text-to-SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11889)
*Anik Pramanik,Murat Kantarcioglu,Vincent Oria,Shantanu Sharma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出DexterSQL，一个基于提示（非微调）的Text-to-SQL系统，通过三个新组件（深度模式探索器、数据库无关规则创建器、多路径SQL生成）解决了现有基于提示方法在模式信息粒度、失败模式捕获和复杂问题条件处理三方面的不足，在BIRD-Dev基准上相较现有最优方法取得了显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提示（非微调）的Text-to-SQL方法面临三个核心问题：(i)依赖粗粒度的模式信息，无法揭示区分模糊列所需的细粒度关系；(ii)未能捕获反复出现的SQL生成失败模式；(iii)在复杂问题中容易出现条件的遗漏、幻觉或错位。本文旨在系统性地解决这些问题。

Method: DexterSQL包含三个创新组件：(1)深度模式探索器——识别模糊列，通过分析其单独及联合数据分布来揭示列间关系及各列独特作用；(2)数据库无关规则创建器——仅在训练数据库上挖掘生成SQL与正确SQL之间的不匹配，并将其转化为数据库无关的纠正规则，以捕获反复出现的LLM失败模式；(3)多路径SQL生成——引入基于依赖树的中间表示，利用问题的句子结构引导其分解为SQL骨架，最终生成完整SQL。

Result: 在BIRD-Dev基准上，DexterSQL在使用开源权重模型GPT-OSS-120B时总准确率达到67.6%，相比现有最优方法提升至少2.7%；在使用闭源权重模型GPT-4o和GPT-5.2时总准确率分别达到71.6%和72.2%，分别提升至少0.9%。

Conclusion: DexterSQL证明了在不进行任何参数微调的前提下，通过深度模式探索、数据库无关规则挖掘和基于依赖树的SQL分解策略，能够有效提升Text-to-SQL的生成准确率，且同时适用于开源和闭源大型语言模型，具有广泛的适用性和显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Prompting-based (\textit{i}.\textit{e}., non-fine-tuning) Text-to-SQL methods, where underlying large language model parameters are not changed for the task, face three problems: (\textit{i})~relying on coarse-grained schema information that may not reveal the fine-grained relationships needed to distinguish ambiguous columns, (\textit{ii})~not capturing recurring SQL-generation failures, and (\textit{iii})~suffering from omission, hallucination, or misplacement of conditions in complex questions.
  This paper develops \textsc{DexterSQL}, a prompting/non-fine-tuning-based Text-to-SQL system that improves SQL generation with three novel components: (\textit{i})~\emph{deep schema explorator} that identifies ambiguous columns, analyzes their individual and joint data distributions to uncover their relationships and the distinct role of each, (\textit{ii})~\emph{database-agnostic rule creator} that mines mismatches between generated and gold SQL only on the training database and converts them into database-agnostic corrective rules that capture recurring LLM failure patterns; and (\textit{iii})~\emph{multi-path SQL generation} that introduces a dependency-tree-based intermediate representation that uses the question's sentence structure to guide its decomposition into an SQL skeleton for final SQL generation.
  \textsc{DexterSQL} achieves a higher accuracy compared to the state-of-the-art using both open-source/weight and closed-source/weight models. Particularly, \textsc{DexterSQL}'s shows a high improvement of at least 2.7\% using an open-weight model (GPT-OSS-120B) on BIRD-Dev, with total accuracy 67.6\%. \textsc{DexterSQL} also shows better improvement of at least 0.9\% using closed-weight models, with total accuracy 71.6\% and 72.2\% on BIRD-Dev with GPT-4o and GPT-5.2.

</details>


### [102] [An Extended Tutorial and Vocabulary for Relational Language Design in an Era of AI-Assisted Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12272)
*Wolfgang Gatterbauer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本教程从实例出发，比较不同关系查询语言在表达同一意图时的结构性差异，并引入抽象关系演算（ARC）和关系图作为参考表示，构建关系语言设计的通用词汇与分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询语言已发展50余年，SQL长期主导实践，但如今查询越来越多由机器生成、人类阅读，同时各类新型查询表示法（数据框、管道、逻辑、函数、图等）不断涌现。现有讨论多关注表达力，却忽视了语言所显式化的关系结构以及符号系统对用户阅读和修改查询的支持程度，这促使作者从结构化和可读性角度比较不同关系语言。

Method: 本教程采用实例驱动而非形式化定义的方式，从示例查询出发比较不同语言如何表达同一意图；使用抽象关系演算（ARC）和关系图作为跨符号系统的参考表示，并据此开发了一套关系语言设计词汇，涵盖信息需求、查询映射、关系模式结构、关系模式指称和语义约定等概念。

Result: 参与者将获得一个用于比较现有及未来关系语言的框架、一套精确阐述设计权衡的词汇表，以及一组连接经典数据库语言与替代方案的实例。

Conclusion: 本教程在SIGMOD'26基础上扩展至递归与路径查询、嵌套关系数据及超越PTIME的关系语言三个新方向，旨在帮助研究者和实践者超越表达力比较，更系统地理解不同关系语言在结构显式化和用户可读性方面的设计取舍。

Abstract: Relational query languages have been studied and used for more than 50 years, with SQL dominant in practice. Today, queries are increasingly generated by machines and read by humans. At the same time, the landscape also includes dataframe, pipeline, logical, functional, graph, and relational programming notations. These developments invite two related questions beyond expressive power: which relational structures do languages make explicit, and how well can notation support users in reading and revising queries?
  This 3-hour tutorial extends an earlier SIGMOD'26 tutorial in three directions: recursive and path queries (connecting relational and graph query languages), nested relational data, and relational languages for problems beyond PTIME. Rather than beginning from formal definitions, we start from example queries and compare how different languages express the same intent. To compare recurring structure across notations, we use Abstract Relational Calculus (ARC) and Relational Diagrams as reference representations.
  From these examples, we develop a vocabulary for relational language design, including information need, query mapping, relational pattern structure, relational pattern denotation, and semantic conventions. Participants will leave with a framework for comparing existing and future relational languages, a precise vocabulary for articulating design trade-offs, and a concrete set of examples connecting classical database languages with alternative proposals.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [TRACES: A Benchmark for Epistemic Reliability in Scientific Reasoning by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11415)
*Valentin Rodionov,Shamil Assylbekov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估框架，用于测试大语言模型在科学工作流中能否识别不可靠的科学文献（如撤稿、造假和伪科学论文）。通过42篇问题论文构建探针语料库，发现所有30个模型在超过71%的探针中失败，22/30的模型失败率超过90%，表明模型缺乏稳健的认知能力，其拒绝行为主要针对高知名度的主题而非真正理解科学内容的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评估模型在已知答案问题上的事实性，未直接测量模型区分可靠与不可靠科学文献的能力。然而LLM正被部署为科学工作流中的智能体，且下游可能缺乏验证器，因此急需评估模型对虚假科学前提的识别能力。

Method: 构建42篇撤稿、造假和伪科学论文的探针语料库，每篇探针将近乎逐字提取的前言与科学上合理的研究设计请求配对。探针涵盖五类说法：伪造观察、伪物理机制、魔法前提、合法性桥梁和货运崇拜实验。使用两个互补分数（IFR-a和IFR-i）测量模型是否直接拒绝错误前提及其是否在识别不可靠性的同时仍参与，并通过Engagement Depth Index（EDI）量化对论文或领域特有细节的复现程度。

Result: 在30个模型和10次重复运行中，聚合IFR-a为0.93±0.004，IFR-i为0.809±0.009。模型在95%的非空响应中参与了站不住脚的前提。每个评估模型在超过71%的智能体探针中失败，22/30的模型超过90%的时间失败。拒绝集中在少数高知名度主题和特定探针上，且在匹配结构控制下消失。

Conclusion: 模型行为符合主题关键词触发的安全行为而非稳健的认知能力，表明需要为语言模型的科学部署建立防护栏基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models are being proposed as agents in scientific workflows, in domains where no downstream verifier exists. Such deployment assumes the model can distinguish reliable scientific literature from unreliable literature, a capability that has not yet been directly measured. Existing benchmarks evaluate factuality on questions with known answers; the failure mode we target here is different. We introduce a probe corpus of 42 retracted, fraudulent, and pseudoscientific papers, paired with a methodology for eliciting and scoring single-shot model engagement with each paper's framing. Each probe pairs a preamble extracted near-verbatim from the target paper with a scientifically plausible study-design request. The probes span five claim types: fabricated observation, pseudophysical mechanism, magical premise, legitimization bridge, and cargo-cult experiment. Two complementary scores measure whether a model rejects the flawed premise outright (IFR-a) and whether it recognizes the unreliability while still engaging (IFR-i). A depth score, the Engagement Depth Index (EDI), quantifies reproduction of paper- or field-specific withheld details. Across 30 models and 10 repeated runs, aggregate IFR-a is 0.93 $\pm$ 0.004 and aggregate IFR-i is 0.809 $\pm$ 0.009. Models engaged with untenable premises in 95% of all non-empty responses. Every evaluated model fails more than 71% of agentic probes, and 22 of 30 models fail more than 90% of the time. Rejections are concentrated on a small number of high-notoriety topics and specific probes, and disappear under matched-structure controls. These results are consistent with topic-keyed safety behavior rather than robust epistemic competence, and indicate an urgent need for guardrail infrastructure for scientific deployment of language models.

</details>


### [104] [From Overlooked to Explored: Recovering Item Relations via Mixture of Perspectives for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11846)
*Junyoung Kim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Jaehyung Lim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM模块，通过多视角注意力校准解决序列推荐中自注意力模型的相似性偏差问题，在七个真实数据集上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制在序列推荐中虽然擅长计算物品间两两交互，但实证分析发现其点积注意力分数存在相似性偏差——过度偏好相似物品，系统性忽视了具有偏好信号的非同质关系，限制了推荐性能。

Method: 提出PRISM（Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module），通过K个视角透镜从不同观点校准注意力。包含Affinity View（细化同质关系）和Contrast View（暴露被相似性偏差抑制的异质关系），从而捕获完整的用户偏好光谱。

Result: 在七个真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PRISM一致性地超越了当前最优的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过多视角关系校准可以有效缓解相似性偏差，让序列推荐模型能够同时捕获同质与异质的物品关系，从而更全面地理解用户偏好。

Abstract: Capturing user preference from a user's interaction sequence is the central challenge of Sequential Recommendation (SR). This preference intuitively emerges from inter-item relations: each item transition reflects a preference embedded in the relations between items, making the faithful capture of these relations essential for accurate recommendation. For this reason, self-attention is dominant in sequential recommendation for its ability to compute pairwise item interactions, yet our empirical analysis reveals that it consistently suffers from similarity bias across various types of transformer-based SR models: dot-product attention scores disproportionately favor similar items, systematically overlooking heterogeneous relations with meaningful preference signals and directly limiting recommendation performance. To address this, we propose PRISM (Perspective-based Relational Insight Synthesis Module), a module that re-examines item relations from multiple perspectives. PRISM employs K Perspective Lenses to calibrate attention from distinct viewpoints, combining an Affinity View that refines homogeneous relations and a Contrast View that exposes heterogeneous ones suppressed by similarity bias, enabling the model to capture the full spectrum of user preferences. Extensive experiments on seven real-world benchmarks demonstrate that PRISM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://github.com/327aem/PRISM/.

</details>


### [105] [Sci-Surf: Navigating Scientific Literature Discovery through Human Feedback and Intelligent Summarizatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11973)
*Fang Guo,Qi Zhu,Rongcan Pei,Shuqi He,Hui Chen,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Sci-Surf是一个以意图为中心的学术发现系统，结合了反馈驱动的个性化推荐与多模态博客式论文解析，通过LLM用户画像提升推荐质量，并在真实用户评估中实现了10.4%的平均预测对齐改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研论文数量激增，现有学术发现平台依赖静态主题订阅或基于嵌入的相似度匹配，仅提供摘要或简短总结，难以支持细粒度意图建模和深度论文理解，缺乏有效的个性化推荐和深度消化工具。

Method: 构建Sci-Surf系统，采用LLM-based用户画像技术细化用户意图表示，同时生成结构化摘要以综合整篇论文的文本和视觉信息，形成端到端的学术发现流水线。

Result: 通过真实用户评估，系统在推荐质量和消化质量上均有可衡量的改进；整合语言化用户画像后，在一个月的在线评估中实现10.4%的平均预测对齐提升。

Conclusion: Sci-Surf验证了意图中心的方法在学术发现中的有效性，将个性化推荐与多模态论文消化相结合，可显著提升学术发现体验。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific publications makes it increasingly difficult for researchers to identify relevant new studies and effectively comprehend them. Existing academic discovery platforms typically rely on static topic subscriptions or embedding-based similarity and provide only abstracts or short summaries, offering limited support for nuanced intent modeling and in-depth paper summarization. We present Sci-Surf, an intent-centric knowledge discovery system that integrates feedback-driven personalized recommendation with multi-modal blog-style paper digestion. Our approach refines user intent representations through LLM-based user profiling, while generating structured summaries that synthesize textual and visual information from full papers. The demo presents an end-to-end academic discovery pipeline and demonstrates measurable improvements in both recommendation quality and digestion quality through real-user evaluations. Specifically, the integration of verbalized profiles led to a 10.4% average improvement in predictive alignment with real-world user preferences throughout a month-long online evaluation.

</details>


### [106] [HCGRec: Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation with Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11980)
*Kangning Zhang,Haotian Fang,Xukun Luo,Hao Yin,Yang Gao,Peng Yan,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HCGRec框架，解决语义ID生成式推荐系统在奖励后训练阶段因早期语义token进入错误分支导致零奖励、无法学习的问题。通过诊断硬实例并注入最小目标前缀提示，将零奖励组转化为信息丰富的比较，同时引入提示感知的信用分解来区分提示token和生成token的优化方式，显著降低了零优势训练样本比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义ID生成式推荐器在奖励后训练中存在结构化优化瓶颈：当早期语义token进入错误分支时，有限的rollout组几乎无法到达目标物品，导致组相对优化收到相同的零奖励，无法产生有效的优势信号。

Method: HCGRec包含三个关键组件：(1) 检查点rollout诊断，识别无法到达正确物品的硬实例；(2) 最小目标前缀提示注入，仅在需要时提供提示，模型在提示分支下生成未提示的后缀；(3) 提示感知的信用分解，对提示token使用监督学习保持物品语义和前缀结构对齐，对未提示token使用GRPO优化采样后缀。

Result: 在序列推荐基准上，HCGRec显著优于监督微调和传统奖励后训练方法，将零优势训练样本从超过70%降低到20%以下。

Conclusion: 提示条件化生成推荐框架有效解决了语义ID推荐中奖励稀疏和零优势问题，通过最小干预的提示策略恢复了硬实例的学习信号，代码已开源。

Abstract: Semantic-ID generative recommenders represent each item as a short sequence of discrete semantic tokens and predict the next item by autoregressively generating this token sequence. This paradigm enables a unified generation interface for item IDs, histories, and item text, but it also creates a structured optimization bottleneck during reward-based post-training: when an early semantic token enters the wrong branch of the item-token space, finite rollout groups rarely reach the ground-truth item, so group-relative optimization receives identical zero rewards and produces no useful advantage. We propose Hint-Conditioned Generative Recommendation (HCGRec), a semantic-ID generative recommendation framework that recovers learning signal for such hard training instances. HCGRec diagnoses each instance with checkpoint rollouts and supplies a minimal target-prefix hint only when the current generator cannot reach the correct item. The model then generates the unhinted suffix under the hinted semantic branch, turning zero-reward groups into informative comparisons over item-token completions. Hinting also changes token identity: hinted prefix tokens are oracle-provided item context, while unhinted suffix tokens are sampled generation actions. We therefore introduce hint-aware credit decomposition, using supervised learning to preserve item-semantic and prefix-structure alignment for hinted tokens and GRPO to optimize the sampled suffix. Experiments on sequential recommendation benchmarks show that HCGRec substantially improves over supervised fine-tuning and vanilla reward-based post-training, while reducing zero-advantage training samples from over 70% to below 20%. The code is accessible at https://github.com/WncFht/GRec.

</details>


### [107] [Token-Level Credit Assignment Optimization for Generative Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12049)
*Xinpeng Zhao,Yang Liu,Ran Chen,Xinyu Ma,Daiting Shi,Pengjie Ren,Zhumin Chen,Zhaochun Ren,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向生成式检索的细粒度强化学习框架，通过估计token级别的相关性奖励来解决传统序列级奖励无法精准分配信用的问题，在检索基准上优于序列级奖励基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索模型的解码过程是序列决策问题，但现有方法仅在整个DocID生成后才评估检索效果，导致token级生成与文档级相关性监督不匹配；序列级奖励将所有解码步等同对待，难以识别哪些token决策导致了检索成功或失败。

Method: 提出细粒度强化学习框架：不将单一奖励分配给整个生成的DocID，而是通过衡量每个token决策对生成轨迹预期检索质量的改变来估计逐步奖励，实现更精确的信用分配；同时发展了针对DocID生成过程的实用奖励估计策略，并将其整合到策略优化框架中。

Result: 在多个检索基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法持续优于使用序列级奖励的基线方法，验证了细粒度监督在将自回归DocID生成与检索目标对齐方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的token级奖励能够更精确地进行信用分配，使策略倾向于选择更能直接贡献于文档级相关性的token决策，从而有效对齐生成式检索中的DocID生成与检索目标。

Abstract: Generative retrieval models perform document retrieval by autoregressively generating document identifiers (DocIDs). This process naturally forms a sequential decision problem, where each decoding step selects a DocID token and the complete token sequence determines the retrieved document. However, retrieval effectiveness is typically evaluated only after the full DocID is generated, creating a mismatch between token-level generation and document-level relevance supervision. As a result, existing reinforcement learning methods for generative retrieval mostly rely on sequence-level rewards, where the same document-level feedback is propagated to all decoding steps. Such coarse-grained feedback makes it difficult to identify which token decisions are responsible for successful or failed retrieval.
  In this work, we propose a fine-grained reinforcement learning framework for generative retrieval with token-level relevance rewards. Instead of assigning a single reward to the entire generated DocID, we estimate step-wise rewards by measuring how each token decision changes the expected retrieval quality of the corresponding generation trajectory. This enables more precise credit assignment and encourages the policy to favor token decisions that contribute more directly to document-level relevance. We further develop practical reward estimation strategies tailored to the DocID generation process and incorporate them into a policy optimization framework. Experiments on retrieval benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms sequence-level reward baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of fine-grained supervision for aligning autoregressive DocID generation with retrieval objectives.

</details>


### [108] [Making Collaborative Signals Count: Graph-Aware Large Language Models for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12184)
*Fenglin Yan,Bohao Wang,Jian Zhang,Yu Cui,Tongya Zheng,Ye Feng,Can Wang,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了GALLM，一种图感知的大语言模型推荐框架，通过构建协作图谱并将图谱关系转化为可学习的注意力偏置嵌入到LLM中，在不引入额外图编码器的情况下实现协作感知的序列推荐，在四个基准数据集上平均HR@5提升9.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐系统主要依赖语言预训练，难以捕捉用户-物品交互中隐含的协作信号。已有方法要么依赖外部推荐器注入协作表示，要么仅建模序列内依赖，无法充分利用全局协作模式。

Method: GALLM构建覆盖文本token和物品token的协作图谱，建模三类关系：文本-文本关系保留语义依赖、物品-文本关系对齐物品token与其文本描述、物品-物品关系捕捉全局物品共现模式；这些关系被转化为轻量级可学习注意力偏置，直接融入LLM注意力机制，无需额外图编码器。

Result: 在四个真实世界基准数据集上，GALLM在所有对比基线中取得最佳性能，平均HR@5较最强基线提升9.76%。

Conclusion: 将全局协作图结构嵌入LLM注意力机制是一种有效且高效的方式，能够弥补LLM在推荐任务中协作信号捕捉不足的缺陷，同时避免了额外图编码器带来的复杂性和开销。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been widely adopted as backbones for recommender systems. However, their language-centric pretraining makes it difficult to capture collaborative signals implicit in user-item interactions, which are crucial for personalized recommendation. Existing methods either inject collaborative representations produced by external recommenders or model only intra-sequence dependencies, limiting their ability to exploit global collaborative patterns. To address this limitation, we propose GALLM, a graph-aware LLM framework for sequential recommendation. GALLM constructs a collaborative graph over text tokens and item tokens, and models three types of relations: Text--Text relations for preserving semantic dependencies, Item--Text relations for aligning item tokens with their textual descriptions, and Item--Item relations derived from global item co-occurrence patterns. These relations are transformed into lightweight learnable attention biases and incorporated into the LLM attention mechanism, enabling collaborative-aware token interactions without introducing an additional graph encoder. Experiments on four real-world benchmarks show that GALLM achieves the best performance among the compared baselines, improving over the strongest baseline by 9.76\% on average in HR@5.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [An Event-Driven Cloud-Native Wearable Analytics Framework for Real-Time Clinical Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11402)
*Elias Grünewald,Daniil Cherepko,Linus Gustafsson,Jakob Möhler,Oskar Rabe,Paul Robin Reichelt,Constantin Stahl,Lukasz Sztukiewicz,Louis Agha-Mir-Salim,Felix Balzer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个事件驱动的云原生系统，用于大规模采集、标准化和分析消费级可穿戴设备的生命体征数据。系统采用多层级微服务架构，支持原生健康框架采集、FHIR标准转换、依赖感知的最小化存储方案以及基于数据湖仓架构的机器学习全生命周期管理，实测可维持每秒50次完整采集请求且中位响应时间低于8毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级可穿戴设备在临床护理和研究中具有巨大潜力，但设备异构性、专有数据格式和严格监管要求严重阻碍了其集成应用，亟需一个可扩展、无供应商锁定且符合监管标准的统一解决方案。

Method: 采用多层级微服务架构配合集群编排；通过跨平台移动应用利用原生健康框架获取数据以兼容碎片化设备生态；利用流处理引擎实现事件驱动的转换管道，将原始测量数据映射到FHIR医疗互操作标准；设计依赖感知的FHIR最小化方案以降低存储开销同时保持无损资源重建；基于medallion湖仓架构构建模块化数据分析与机器学习层，覆盖从实时流处理到模型服务的完整ML生命周期。

Result: 性能评估显示，采集管道可维持每秒50个完整采集请求，中位响应时间低于8毫秒，满足实时患者监测的低延迟需求。系统通过基于角色的访问控制和安全的服务间通信符合监管合规标准。

Conclusion: 该开源系统为机构医疗环境中的可穿戴设备监测部署提供了稳健基础，可支持临床决策辅助和研究工作负载，有效解决了设备异构、互操作性和监管合规等核心挑战。

Abstract: Continuous physiological monitoring using consumer-grade wearables offers a transformative opportunity for clinical care and research, yet integration remains hindered by device heterogeneity, proprietary data formats, and strict regulatory requirements. We present an event-driven, cloud-native system designed to ingest, normalize, and analyze high-frequency vital signs from wearables at scale and without vendor lock-in. The system design proposes a multi-layered microservice architecture using cluster orchestration. Data acquisition is handled via a cross-platform mobile application that leverages native health frameworks, ensuring compatibility across fragmented device ecosystems. To address interoperability, we implement an event-driven transformation pipeline using stream processing engines and specialized services to map raw measurements to the FHIR standard for medical interoperability. Our novel dependency-aware FHIR minimization scheme reduces storage overhead while maintaining lossless resource reconstruction. Furthermore, the platform integrates a modular data analytics and machine learning layer based on a medallion lakehouse architecture, supporting the full machine learning lifecycle from real-time stream processing to model serving. Performance evaluation demonstrates that the ingestion pipeline sustains 50 full ingestion requests per second with median response times under 8 ms, satisfying the low-latency requirements for real-time patient monitoring. Our open-source implementation adheres to regulatory compliance standards through role-based access control and secure service-to-service communication, providing a robust foundation for deploying wearable-based monitoring in institutional healthcare settings for clinical decision support and research workloads.

</details>


### [110] [Descriptive Dispatch of Computational Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11524)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了AI/ML驱动的调度代理在多集群环境中的可靠性，通过432次运行测试了五个特征维度与四种提示风格的所有组合，代理成功率达97.9%。在多集群完整编排实验中，描述性元数据将220个提交作业的成功执行率从48%提升至87%，消除了架构不匹配问题，并让五个应用性能提升最多3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学工作流在多集群环境中运行面临动态作业转换、调度和向异构集群提交的重大挑战，需要可靠的自动化解决方案来提升作业执行的效率与成功率。

Method: 构建了一款调度代理（dispatch agent），能够接收文本指令、准备作业规格并完成提交。通过432次运行系统测试五个特征维度与四种提示风格的所有组合，并在多集群场景中运行完整的编排流程（提交、排队、匹配、评分、选择、转换、调度）。

Result: 调度代理在432次运行中达到97.9%的高成功率。在220个提交作业的多集群实验中，描述性元数据使成功执行率从48%提升至87%，完全消除了架构不匹配问题，并提升了十个可测量应用中五个的性能，最高提升3.3倍。

Conclusion: AI/ML代理能够可靠地处理多集群环境中的作业调度任务，描述性元数据的引入显著提升了作业执行成功率和应用性能，证明了基于代理的调度方案在大规模科学工作流编排中的实用价值。

Abstract: Agents powered by AI/ML are becoming ingrained in orchestration. Dispatch of work is the task of receiving a request, transforming it for a workload manager, and successfully submitting it. Running scientific workflows across multi-cluster environments introduces substantial challenges of dynamic job transformation, dispatch, and submission to heterogeneous clusters. These tasks are well-suited to agents, which can receive textual instructions for work, prepare job specifications, and dispatch. In this work, we assess the reliability of a dispatch agent across 432 runs, testing all possible combinations of five feature dimensions across four prompt styles. The agent is highly reliable (97.9% success). We test a full orchestration to submit, queue, match, score, select, transform, and dispatch in a multi-cluster experiment. We find that descriptive metadata increases successful execution from 48% to 87% of 220 submitted jobs, eliminating architecture mismatch, and improving performance for five of ten measurable applications by up to 3.3x.

</details>


### [111] [User-Assisted Collaborative Distributed Inference for Efficient QoS-Aware Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11840)
*Alfreds Lapkovskis,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协作式分布式推理系统，将专用基础设施与用户贡献的资源相结合，用于AI推理服务的可扩展部署。通过高维生成马尔可夫模型捕捉用户、资源、任务和策略之间的随机动态交互，模拟实验表明随着用户规模增长，分布式调度在请求完成率、P99延迟和专用资源消耗方面均优于集中式调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理服务需求不断增长，但集中式服务架构的成本随需求线性上升，缺乏可扩展且成本高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出协作式分布式推理系统，结合专用基础设施（提供QoS基线容量）与用户贡献的志愿资源（吸收增长需求）；开发具有结构化时间分解的高维生成马尔可夫模型，支持仿真并为任务调度及QoS感知资源分配优化提供基础。

Result: 仿真结果表明，随着用户群体规模增长，分布式调度优势愈发明显，可改善请求完成率和P99延迟，同时显著降低专用资源消耗。

Conclusion: 用户辅助的协作推理能够实现基础设施高效的自动扩展，验证了协作式分布式推理的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Growing demand for artificial intelligence (AI) inference services requires scalable infrastructure, yet centralized serving costs rise with demand. We propose a collaborative distributed inference system combining dedicated infrastructure with resources contributed by service users. Dedicated resources provide baseline capacity for maintaining quality of service (QoS), while volunteered resources absorb increasing demand without proportional growth in centralized infrastructure. To capture stochastic and dynamic interactions among users, resources, tasks, and policies, we develop a high-dimensional generative Markov model with structured temporal factorization. The model supports simulation and provides a foundation for task scheduling and QoS-aware resource allocation optimization. We evaluate the system across user populations, resource capacities, and centralized and distributed scheduling policies. Simulations show that distributed scheduling becomes increasingly advantageous as the user population grows, improving request completion and P99 latency while substantially reducing dedicated resource consumption. These results demonstrate the feasibility of user-assisted collaborative inference for infrastructure-efficient autoscaling.

</details>


### [112] [Evaluating OpenMP Offloading for Intra-node Multi-GPU Programming across NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel Architectures: A 3D Heat Transfer Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11882)
*Ezhilmathi Krishnasamy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了OpenMP Offloading编程模型在单节点多GPU（NVIDIA、AMD、Intel）环境下求解3D热方程的收益与性能挑战，研究表明多GPU相比单GPU可实现约2x（2 GPU）和4x（4 GPU）的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级计算机单个计算节点通常配备多块GPU（4块或更多），但有效利用这些GPU面临数据通信、编程模型选择以及跨不同GPU架构保持单一代码库性能等挑战，需要系统性分析OpenMP Offloading在异构多GPU环境下的适用性。

Method: 以3D热方程为测试用例（包含主计算、halo计算和通信），在NVIDIA、AMD、Intel三种GPU上使用OpenMP Offloading实现，分别采用不同的底层API进行内存分配和传输（同步、异步、peer-to-peer），并与各自的原生编程模型（CUDA、HIP、SYCL）进行性能对比。

Result: OpenMP Offloading在所有三种GPU上相比单GPU实现，2 GPU时性能提升约2倍，4 GPU时提升约4倍；OpenMP Offloading的性能与各GPU特有的原生编程模型（CUDA、HIP、SYCL）的性能进行了系统性对比分析。

Conclusion: OpenMP Offloading作为一种指令驱动的编程模型，能够在不同GPU架构上以单一代码库实现近线性扩展的多GPU性能加速，是一种可行且高效的异构多GPU科学计算方案。

Abstract: Currently, most supercomputers are equipped with GPUs from manufacturers such as NVIDIA, AMD, or Intel, which provide substantial parallelism and high throughput. It is common for a single compute node (intra-node) to host multiple GPUs, typically four or more. Therefore, effectively leveraging all these GPUs within a single compute node is essential for applications in scientific and engineering domains. However, several factors must be considered before utilizing these GPUs for scientific computing, including the implementation of data communication, the programming models available for use across these GPUs, and the level of performance that can be achieved with a single codebase across different GPU architectures and configurations within a single compute node. OpenMP Offloading is a prominent directive-based programming model that can be executed on all three GPU types: NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel. In this research, we present an analysis of the benefits and performance challenges of using OpenMP Offloading to address the 3D heat equations, which involve both primary computation, as well as halo computation and communication. We investigate how performance varies in relation to native GPU programming models--CUDA for NVIDIA, HIP for AMD, and SYCL for Intel. Furthermore, we demonstrate that OpenMP Offloading can achieve performance improvements of approximately 2x for 2 GPUs and around 4x for 4 GPUs when compared to single-GPU OpenMP Offloading implementations across all three GPU types. This analysis is conducted systematically through various OpenMP Offloading implementations that utilize different low-level APIs for memory allocation, memory transfer options (synchronous, asynchronous, and peer-to-peer), and other native GPU programming models such as CUDA (NVIDIA), HIP (AMD), and SYCL (Intel)

</details>


### [113] [Ready Cohorts: Bounding GPU Opportunity and Avoiding Host Round Trips in LLM-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12123)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体服务中控制路径的GPU并发执行机会，提出了就绪队列边界的形式化度量（固定分区份额F、精确离线份额P*、局部上界U、在线达成份额A），并通过动态规划精确计算P*，实验证明精确打包可恢复81.83%的固定窗口边界损失；同时研究了将GPU计算的决策保留在设备端而非返回主机的机制，在全部36种配置中设备驻留路径均更快（1.19x–2.39x），并建立了两个可测量的GPU代理控制门：截止时间可行的队列供应和观测放置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体服务需要反复执行模型与工具调用之间的小型确定性转换（路由结果、更新状态、发出下一效果），研究者希望探究这一控制路径何时能暴露足够的并发工作以供GPU执行，以及GPU计算的路径决策保留在设备上时会带来什么变化。

Method: 形式化定义了就绪队列边界，使用固定分区份额F、精确离线份额P*、局部上界U和在线达成份额A四个指标；在零服务时间、无限容量、等相对发布截止时间假设下，用专用动态规划精确计算P*；在固定851会话公共轨迹面板的平稳泊松重放上进行实验；另设计机制研究，将GPU计算的二元决策保留在设备端而非返回4字节给主机再重新调度。

Result: 在100,000个目标活跃会话、K=256、50ms发布截止时间条件下，F=30.19%、P*=43.00%、U=45.85%，精确打包恢复了81.83%的固定窗口边界机会损失；设备驻留路径在全部36种配置中均快于主机往返，行中位数比率在1.19x至2.39x之间；两个可接受机制下，全部14,557,440次批量调用与独立主机预言机一致；去除主机决策的固定嵌套设备图在5种放置的60种配置中全部更慢。

Conclusion: 研究建立了GPU代理控制的两个可测量门：截止时间可行的队列供应和观测放置。要测量A、CPU位移和服务级收益，需要构建联合的有限在线运行时。

Abstract: LLM-agent services repeatedly execute small deterministic transitions between model and tool calls: route an outcome, update state, and emit the next effect. We ask when this control path exposes enough concurrent work for GPU execution, and what changes when a GPU-computed route decision remains on device. We formalize the ready-cohort boundary using fixed-partition share F, exact offline share P*, local upper bound U, and online achieved share A. Under zero service time, unlimited capacity, and equal relative launch deadlines, a specialized dynamic program computes P* exactly. In a stationary Poisson replay of one pinned 851-session public trace panel, the primary condition at 100,000 target active sessions, K=256, and a 50 ms launch deadline gives F=30.19%, P*=43.00%, and U=45.85%. Exact packing recovers 81.83% of the opportunity lost at fixed window boundaries. The outcome-derived route key is a conditioning proxy, not proof of executable identity. A separate mechanism study keeps a GPU-computed binary decision on device instead of returning four bytes to the host and redispatching. Across four named GPU placements, the device-resident path is faster in all 36 configurations; within-placement row-median ratios range from 1.19x to 2.39x. Across both admissible mechanisms, all 14,557,440 tested batched invocations match a separately implemented host oracle. A fixed nested device graph that removes no host decision is slower in all 60 configurations across five placements. Together, the studies establish two measurable gates for GPU agent control: deadline-feasible cohort supply and observation placement. A joined finite online runtime is required to measure A, CPU displacement, and service-level benefit.

</details>


### [114] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个面向MoE强化学习训练的分层规划器，通过联合协调层级的专家重路由与注意力/专家感知的数据打包，在优化器步窗口内平衡密集注意力和稀疏专家工作负载，在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上分别提升训练吞吐8.85%和14.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的强化学习训练中，序列组合（决定数据并行微批次中的密集注意力工作）和token路由（决定专家并行秩上的稀疏专家工作）这两个负载均衡问题相互耦合，单独优化其中一个可能将瓶颈转移到另一个。现有方法缺乏对这两个问题的联合优化。

Method: RoutePack采用分层规划方案：首先在每个MoE层基于聚合路由需求独立放置专家；然后将样本打包到最小可认证的token上限执行行数中，并通过投影EDP分片感知目标函数优化DP布局。该目标综合窗口归一化线性二次注意力代理和每层物理EP秩峰值，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积成本。并行群体退火搜索保持固定行的可行布局，同时保留样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、等微批次计数和通信拓扑。状态一致的物化保持了逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均训练器测量token吞吐3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%。总体提升分别达到8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过分层规划联合优化专家重路由和数据打包，有效解决了MoE RL中注意力与专家工作负载耦合的瓶颈问题，在两个模型规模上均验证了显著的吞吐提升效果。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [MergirafSemi: A Language-Agnostic Semistructured Merge Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11345)
*Pedro Lopes,Paulo Borba,Paola Accioly,Guilherme Cavalcanti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MergirafSemi，一种语言无关的半结构化合并工具，利用轻量级具体语法树（CST）引导合并决策，在不依赖完整结构化建模或语言专用实现的情况下，在特定程序区域（如方法体）内进行行级合并。实证研究表明，该工具在减少虚假冲突的同时保持竞争性准确性和更高的运行时效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在集成并发更改时频繁遭遇合并冲突，现有行级合并工具产生大量虚假冲突或遗漏真实冲突，而全结构化工具计算成本高、语言专用工具开发和维护成本大，需要一种平衡各方案优劣的合并方法。

Method: 提出MergirafSemi，一种语言无关的半结构化合并工具，利用轻量级具体语法树（CST）捕获结构信息，无需完整结构建模或语言专用实现，并在特定程序区域（如Java方法体）内应用行级合并策略，在准确性和效率之间取得平衡。

Result: 实证研究（涵盖多种编程语言的真实合并场景）表明：增加结构粒度可改善自动冲突解决但可能增加遗漏真实冲突的风险；MergirafSemi实现了更均衡的权衡，显著减少虚假冲突，保持竞争性准确度，且相比非结构化工具大幅降低虚假冲突，相比语言专用的半结构化工具具有相当效果、更稳健的执行行为和显著更低的运行时开销。

Conclusion: 半结构化合并方法在减少虚假冲突与保持合并准确性之间可以取得良好平衡，MergirafSemi证明了语言无关的半结构化方法在效率和效果上具有实际可行性，为后续合并工具设计提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Developers frequently face merge conflicts when integrating concurrent changes. Most merge tools rely on unstructured, line-based comparisons, often producing spurious conflicts and missing actual conflicts. To address these limitations, structure-aware merge tools have been proposed, which leverage syntactic representations to improve merge accuracy. However, fully structured tools may incur higher computational cost, and language-specific tools require significant development and maintenance effort. To balance these trade-offs, we propose MergirafSemi, a language-agnostic semistructured merge tool that captures structural information without requiring full structural modeling or language-specific implementations, and applies line-based merging within specific program regions, such as method bodies in Java. Our tool leverages lightweight Concrete Syntax Trees to guide merging decisions while preserving flexibility and efficiency across languages. We evaluate our tool through an empirical study on real-world merge scenarios across multiple programming languages, comparing it with unstructured, semistructured, and structured tools. Our results show that increasing structural granularity improves automatic conflict resolution but can also lead to more aggressive merge decisions, increasing the number of missed actual conflicts. In contrast, MergirafSemi achieves a more balanced trade-off, reducing spurious conflicts while maintaining competitive accuracy and better runtime performance in most common scenarios. Compared to an unstructured tool, it substantially reduces spurious conflicts, and when compared to a semistructured language-specific tool, it achieves comparable effectiveness while exhibiting more robust execution behavior and significantly lower runtime overhead.

</details>


### [116] [The Devil Is in the Interface: Evaluating How Tool Architecture Shapes Coding Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11386)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hamidreza Saghir,Qianhui Wu,Marc-Alexandre Côté,Tong Wang,Kiran Lakkaraju,Kexin Pei,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了编码智能体中的"工具架构"（tool architecture）设计维度，通过受控实验比较六种工具架构在仓库级问题修复任务中的表现，发现即使工具能力相似，不同的组织与暴露方式也会显著改变智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于智能体工具的研究主要关注扩展智能体的能力（即"能做什么"），但较少系统性地关注这些能力如何被组织和暴露给模型。作者将这一设计维度定义为"工具架构"，并认为它对智能体的行为表现有重要影响，值得系统研究。

Method: 在仓库级问题修复任务上开展受控实验，保持底层信息和动作相似，仅改变工具的组织和暴露方式。比较六种工具架构，涉及三个不同智能体（actors），总计11,700条轨迹数据。

Result: 1）相比仅提供bash工具的基础架构，结构化的底层接口可将重复尝试的一致性提升多达4.7倍；2）自然语言搜索能拓宽仓库探索范围，对相关文件的访问增加超过11%；3）Python CodeAct风格的接口在相似任务性能下，步骤数减少41.6%，token使用量降低56.3%；4）轻量级文本认知脚手架工具（如记录中间推理的工具）对智能体行为影响有限。

Conclusion: 工具架构是智能体系统设计中的关键维度，不同的架构选择即使在能力等价的情况下也会显著影响智能体的行为模式与效率。这提示研究者和工程师在构建智能体系统时，应像重视工具能力一样重视工具的组织与暴露方式。

Abstract: As large language models continue to improve, agentic systems are becoming increasingly important, and tools are a key design dimension because they determine how agents access information and take action in their environments. Prior work on agent tooling has primarily focused on expanding what agents can do, but has paid less systematic attention to how those capabilities are organized and exposed to the model. We refer to this latter design dimension as tool architecture. We study tool architecture in coding agents through controlled experiments on repository-level issue fixing, comparing six tool architectures that hold the underlying information and actions similar while varying how they are organized and exposed to the model, across three actors and a total of 11,700 trajectories. Our experiments show that, even when tools provide similar capabilities, tool architecture changes agent behavior: Compared to a basic architecture where the agent has only the bash tool, more structured low-level interfaces improve consistency across repeated attempts by up to 4.7 $\times$; natural-language search broadens repository exploration and increases access to relevant files by more than 11%; and Python CodeAct-style interfaces achieve similar task performance with 41.6% fewer steps and 56.3% lower token usage. By contrast, lightweight text-based cognitive-scaffolding tools, such as tools that let the agent record intermediate reasoning, have limited effect on actor behavior.

</details>


### [117] [GraphAlignCoder: Aligning Program and Proof Graphs for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11394)
*Yueke Zhang,Zihan Fang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphAlignCoder，一种通过在代码生成中注入显式正确性结构（实现图和证明流图）来训练代码大语言模型的框架，显著提升了模型在多个基准上的解题能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的执行反馈训练方法只能判断程序是否失败，无法提供关于正确解决方案应如何组织的细粒度监督信号，导致代码LLM虽能生成语法合理的程序却违反隐藏的语义约束。

Method: GraphAlignCoder构建实现图（捕捉程序区域间的控制和依赖关系），同时通过受限Lean流水线生成证明轨迹并提取形式化证明流图。模型首先学习可执行代码及图派生的正确性描述，然后将这些知识整合到代码生成中。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6上解题数从38提升到50（相对提升31.6%），在BigCodeBench Hard上从16提升到23（相对提升43.8%），BigCodeBench Full从359提升到363，全面超越基础模型、纯代码SFT和CodeRL。

Conclusion: 验证图注入提供了初始推理增益，而验证到代码的整合对跨基准稳健迁移至关重要，验证了将显式正确性结构注入代码生成训练的有效性。

Abstract: Code large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically plausible programs that nevertheless violate hidden semantic constraints. Existing execution-feedback training methods identify whether a completed program fails, but provide limited supervision about how a correct solution should be organized. We introduce GraphAlignCoder, a training framework that transfers explicit correctness structure into code generation.
  GraphAlignCoder constructs an implementation graph that captures control and dependence among program regions. In parallel, a constrained Lean pipeline produces proof traces, from which we extract a formal proof-flow graph. The model first learns executable code together with graph-derived descriptions of why individual program regions are correct, and then consolidates this knowledge into code generation. GraphAlignCoder consistently outperforms the base model, code-only SFT, and CodeRL across all benchmarks. Compared with CodeRL, it increases the solved count from 38 to 50 on LiveCodeBench v6 and from 16 to 23 on BigCodeBench Hard, corresponding to relative gains of 31.6% and 43.8%, while also improving BigCodeBench Full from 359 to 363 tasks. The ablation study further shows that verification-graph injection produces the initial reasoning gain, while verification to code consolidation is essential for robust cross-benchmark transfer.

</details>


### [118] [Simplifying Requirements Engineering in the Context of the LGPD: An LLM-Based Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11454)
*Cinara Gomes de Melo Carneiro,Renato de Freitas Bulcão Neto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在巴西《通用数据保护法》（LGPD）框架下，从现行立法自动生成用户故事和验收测试场景，以简化需求工程（RE）流程，评估结果显示高性能，证实了LLMs在软件初期确保监管合规的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私立法合规对需求工程构成复杂挑战——将法律规范转化为软件需求的过程繁琐且易出错，亟需自动化手段简化这一转化过程。

Method: 提出一种利用当前隐私立法（LGPD）作为输入，借助大型语言模型自动生成用户故事（User Stories）和验收测试场景（Acceptance Test Scenarios）的方法。

Result: 评估结果显示该方法表现出高性能，验证了LLMs在从软件初始阶段确保监管合规方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LLMs能够有效简化需求工程中的法律合规流程，具备从软件设计源头保障隐私法规遵从的潜力。

Abstract: Compliance with privacy legislation poses a complex challenge to Requirements Engineering (RE): translating legal norms into software requirements. In this context, this study investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can simplify RE within the framework of the Brazilian General Data Protection Law (LGPD). The proposed approach utilizes current legislation to automatically generate User Stories and Acceptance Test Scenarios. The evaluation results demonstrated high performance, confirming the potential of LLMs to ensure regulatory compliance from the software's inception.

</details>


### [119] [Do Influence Tactics Matter? Investigating Prompt Framing Effects in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11513)
*Alex Deaconu,Anubhav Gupta,Manaal Basha,Nicholas Haydu,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次大规模实证探讨了心理学启发的影响力提示词框架（如理性说服、奉承、交换等八种策略）对LLM在代码生成任务中的影响，发现某些策略（特别是强调紧迫性的）会降低代码正确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然提示词的措辞和结构已知会影响LLM性能，但基于心理学影响力策略的提示词框架对LLM在编程任务中的行为影响尚未被探索。

Method: 基于Yukl & Falbe的八种影响力策略（如理性说服、奉承、交换等），将其操作化为可复现的提示词模板，在五个领先的开源LLM上使用LiveCodeBench和SWE-bench Verified两个基准进行评测，从功能正确性、质量、可维护性和安全性四个维度评估代码输出。

Result: 某些影响力诱导的提示词框架，特别是强调紧迫性的策略，与降低的正确性和安全性相关。这是首个关于影响力提示词框架在软件工程任务中的大规模实证研究。

Conclusion: 语言线索会显著影响LLM代码生成输出，研究为设计透明和可解释的人机交互代码生成提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software engineering workflows, helping developers write, debug, test, and maintain code. While prompt wording and structure are known to influence model performance, the impact of psychologically inspired prompt framings remains unexplored. This study investigates whether different psychology-based communication strategies that humans use to persuade or motivate others can lead to more effective prompt framing, which may, in turn, affect LLM behaviour in coding tasks. Drawing on Yukl & Falbe's well-known taxonomy, we operationalized eight influence tactics (like rational persuasion, ingratiation, and exchange) into reproducible prompt templates. These prompt templates were evaluated across five leading open-weight LLMs using two widely adopted benchmarks: LiveCodeBench and SWE-bench Verified. We assessed the resulting code output on four key software quality dimensions: functional correctness, quality, maintainability, and security. Our results show that certain influence-induced prompt framings, particularly those emphasizing urgency, were associated with reduced correctness and security. This work presents the first large-scale empirical study of influence-induced prompt framing in software engineering tasks, offering insights into how linguistic cues may shape LLM outputs. We conclude with practical insights for designing transparent and interpretable human-AI interactions in code generation.

</details>


### [120] [Harnessing LLMs for Document-Guided Fuzzing of Python Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11744)
*Bin Duan,Tarek Mahmud,Meiru Che,Yan Yan,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VistaFuzz，一种基于文档引导的模糊测试技术，利用本地开源LLM从API文档中提取参数规范并生成满足参数约束及参数间依赖关系的输入。在12个Python库的7,718个API上评估，发现40.1%的API存在参数间依赖关系，禁用依赖解析会使有效生成率从95%以上降至31.6%-52.8%。共报告74个问题，43个已获开发者确认，29个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python库在深度学习、科学计算等领域至关重要，其API测试需要满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的输入。现有方法要么将约束隐含在生成程序中，要么依赖库特定的解析规则，缺乏通用且有效的约束提取与满足方案。

Method: VistaFuzz采用文档引导的模糊测试技术，通过本地部署的开源LLM从API文档中提取参数规范，并据此生成同时满足参数约束和参数间依赖关系的测试输入。

Result: 在12个Python库的7,718个API上进行评估。参数间关系在40.1%的测试API中出现，禁用依赖解析后有效生成率从95%以上降至31.6%-52.8%。VistaFuzz报告了74个问题，其中43个被开发者确认，29个已修复。

Conclusion: VistaFuzz通过LLM驱动的文档解析与输入生成，有效解决了Python API测试中参数依赖关系难以处理的问题，显著提升了测试输入的有效生成率，并在实际库中发现了大量被开发者确认的真实问题。

Abstract: Python libraries underpin deep learning, scientific computing, data analysis, and computer vision, making their reliability critical to downstream applications. Testing their APIs requires inputs that satisfy both per-parameter constraints and dependencies among parameters. Existing approaches either leave such constraints implicit in generated programs or rely on library-specific parsing rules. This paper introduces VistaFuzz, a document-guided fuzzing technique that uses a locally served open-sourced LLM to extract parameter specifications from API documents and generate inputs that satisfy both parameter constraints and inter-parameter dependencies. We evaluate VistaFuzz on 7,718 APIs across twelve Python libraries. Inter-parameter relationships occur in 40.1\% of tested APIs, and disabling their resolution reduces the valid generation rate on those APIs from above 95\% to 31.6\%--52.8\%. VistaFuzz reports 74 issues, of which 43 have been confirmed by developers and 29 have been fixed.

</details>


### [121] [Instruction Alignment for Binary Code Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11766)
*Huaijin Wang,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用指令对齐知识作为辅助训练目标来改进二进制代码表示学习，实验证明该方法能提升检索准确率并为相似性判断提供更具判别力的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制代码表示学习方法主要学习函数级嵌入，捕获粗粒度的语义关系，忽略了细粒度的指令级对应关系，从而错失了编译器调试信息中提供的宝贵监督信号。

Method: 初步研究发现函数级二进制代码相似性微调模型比预训练模型具有更好的指令对齐能力，表明指令对齐与函数级嵌入质量存在强相关性。基于此观察，设计训练方法将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标显式纳入。

Result: 实验表明，指令对齐训练提升了检索准确率，并为模型的相似性判断提供了更具判别力的信号。

Conclusion: 将指令对齐作为辅助训练目标可以有效改进二进制代码表示学习，获得更准确且更具可解释性的代码表示。

Abstract: Binary code representation learning is a fundamental problem in software security and reverse engineering. Existing methods mainly learn function-level embeddings that capture coarse-grained semantic relationships between binary functions, but they largely ignore fine-grained instruction-level correspondences. This limitation misses valuable supervision signals available from compiler debug information, which can support the learning of more accurate and interpretable binary code representations.
  We propose to leverage instruction alignment knowledge to further improve binary code representation learning. Our preliminary study reveals that models finetuned for function-level binary code similarity exhibit substantially better instruction alignment than their pre-trained model, suggesting a strong correlation between instruction alignment and function-level embedding quality. Motivated by this observation, we design a training approach that explicitly incorporates instruction alignment as an auxiliary training objective. Our experiments show that instruction alignment training improves retrieval accuracy and provides more discriminative signal for the model's similarity judgments.

</details>


### [122] [Testing Deep Learning Library APIs via Cross-Framework Differential Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11886)
*Bin Duan,Ruican Dong,Naipeng Dong,Dan Dongseong Kim,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Xamt，一种针对深度学习库API的跨框架差分模糊测试方法。Xamt在七个深度学习库中构建了676个执行验证的API等价组（含2,563个匹配API），并通过方差引导的差分模糊测试识别出72个可独立复现的差异案例（含4个崩溃和68个输出不一致），其中25个已获开发者确认、23个已修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习库API级测试技术通常依赖库内属性或CPU-GPU差分预言机，可能遗漏在不同硬件后端上行为一致的缺陷。需要一种跨框架的差分测试方法，以发现那些在单库或单硬件环境下难以察觉的API实现差异问题。

Method: Xamt采用跨框架差分模糊测试方法：（1）利用显式API别名和参数角色归一化构建候选API对应关系；（2）通过成对执行和基于规范普通输入的分组级行为检查，验证并构建执行有效的API等价组；（3）使用方差引导的差分模糊测试，覆盖普通、边界和非有限输入；（4）通过崩溃预言机和输出不一致预言机标记异常终止或输出不一致的执行，供后续复现和分析。

Result: 在七个深度学习库中，Xamt构建了676个执行验证的API等价组，包含2,563个匹配API；识别出72个可独立复现的差异案例（4个崩溃、68个输出不一致）；在72份开发者报告中，25个已获确认，其中23个已被修复。

Conclusion: Xamt通过跨框架差分模糊测试有效发现了深度学习库API实现中的缺陷，证明了该方法在识别跨库API行为不一致方面的实用性，且发现的缺陷得到了开发者的广泛确认和修复。

Abstract: Deep learning libraries underpin many safety- and reliability-critical applications, yet existing API-level testing techniques often rely on intra-library properties or CPU--GPU differential oracles and may miss defects that behave consistently across hardware backends. We present Xamt, a cross-framework differential fuzzing approach for deep learning library APIs. Xamt constructs and tests execution-validated groups of APIs intended to implement equivalent operations across seven libraries. It uses explicit API aliases and parameter-role normalization to construct candidate correspondences and validates them through pairwise execution and a group-level behavioral check on canonical ordinary inputs. The resulting groups are explored using variance-guided differential fuzzing with ordinary, boundary, and non-finite inputs. Crash and inconsistency oracles flag executions exhibiting abnormal termination or inconsistent outputs for subsequent reproduction and analysis. Across the seven libraries, Xamt constructs 676 execution-validated groups containing 2,563 matched APIs. Among these, Xamt identifies 72 independently reproduced discrepancy cases, including 4 crash cases and 68 output inconsistencies. Among the 72 developer reports, 25 have been confirmed, including 23 that have been fixed.

</details>


### [123] [Developing LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems in Software Engineering: A Mixed-Method Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11965)
*Mariama Celi Serafim De Oliveira,Motunrayo Osatohanmen Ibiyo,Marco Gianrusso,Claudio Di Sipio,Davide Di Ruscio,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件工程中用于实现多智能体系统（MAS）的现有工具和框架进行了全面综述，通过定量分析评估了最相关的开源MAS框架，并通过实现README.md文件摘要任务对选定的框架进行了定性评估。研究结果表明，所选框架对MAS的基本组件覆盖良好，但代理遥测等高级功能仍然缺失；在ROUGE分数方面，不同框架在摘要任务上没有显著差异。最后提供了经验教训和挑战，帮助研究人员和从业者根据需求选择合适的MAS框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统（MAS）在软件工程中的广泛应用，开发者在选择合适技术、制定协调规则和设计代理角色等方面面临诸多挑战。目前缺乏对现有MAS框架的系统性评估，研究者需要一个全面的分析来帮助开发者做出明智的技术选型决策。

Method: 本文采用了两阶段的评估方法：首先，从开发者视角对最相关的开源MAS框架进行了定量分析，评估其文档、特性和能力；其次，对选定的框架子集进行了定性评估，通过实现一个共同的用例（README.md文件的摘要生成）来比较框架的实际表现。

Result: 研究发现所选框架对MAS的基本组件覆盖良好，但诸如代理遥测等高级功能仍然缺失。在README.md摘要任务中，各框架在ROUGE分数上没有显著差异。此外，作者基于实践经验提供了一套经验教训和挑战清单。

Conclusion: 尽管现有MAS框架已能较好支持软件工程中的多智能体协作，但在高级功能方面仍有改进空间。研究者提出了一套经验教训和挑战，可作为研究人员和从业者选择合适MAS框架的参考指南，帮助其根据具体需求做出更明智的决策。

Abstract: The proliferation of Generative Artificial Intelligence (Gen AI) powered by large language models (LLMs) has transformed the software development process, introducing new paradigms for code generation, debugging, testing, and maintenance. While early applications focused on leveraging single, independent LLMs to assist developers with isolated tasks, recent advances have shifted toward multi-agent systems (MAS) that orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents working collaboratively toward common objectives. Despite their promising potential, using MAS encompasses a set of challenges for developers who have to carefully select the right technology, devise proper coordination rules, and design specific roles for the involved agents. In this paper, we provide a comprehensive overview of the existing tools and frameworks for implementing MAS in software engineering. First, we conducted a quantitative analysis of the most relevant open source MAS frameworks by evaluating their documentation, features, and capabilities from the developers' perspective. Second, we performed a qualitative evaluation of a subset of the selected frameworks by implementing a common use case: the summarization of README.MD files. The findings show that the selected frameworks provide a good coverage of fundamental components of MAS, though advanced features such as telemetry of agents are still missing. In addition, the empirical evaluation shows that there is no significant difference in terms of ROUGE scores considering the summarization task. Finally, we provide a set of lessons learned and challenges that can help researchers and practitioners to select a suitable MAS framework according to their needs.

</details>


### [124] [RealisticTritonBench: A Benchmark for Triton-Kernel Generation in Real-World AI Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12004)
*Jinjun Huang,Zhongzhen Wen,Tongtong Xu,Meng Yan,Xin Xia,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了RealisticTritonBench，首个从真实AI框架Pull Request中提取Triton内核生成任务的基准测试，克服了现有基准仅关注PyTorch转Triton翻译、仅评估单个内核性能以及依赖可能存在缺陷的手动评估脚本三大局限，并通过端到端测试实现更真实的评估。实验发现主流LLM在此基准上仍难以完成真实世界的Triton内核生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成Triton内核的基准测试存在三个关键局限：任务仅限PyTorch-to-Triton翻译，无法反映真实世界的多样性和复杂性；只评估单个内核性能而非端到端性能（这是AI框架实际部署的核心标准）；依赖手动编写且可能存在缺陷的评估脚本，模型可能利用这些缺陷绕过正确性检查获得虚高的分数。

Method: RealisticTritonBench从流行的开源AI框架中系统性提取修改Triton内核的Pull Request，将其转化为带具体工程上下文的生成任务。每个任务以自然语言需求为输入，要求生成对应的Triton内核实现，并提供完整可复现的评估环境。与以往仅关注孤立内核性能的基准不同，该方法将生成的内核集成回原始框架，使用端到端测试进行评估。

Result: 在RealisticTritonBench上对主流LLM进行评估，结果显示它们在真实世界的Triton内核生成任务上仍然表现困难，说明现有模型在该更具挑战性和真实性的基准上能力不足。

Conclusion: RealisticTritonBench填补了LLM生成Triton内核评估中真实性与端到端性能评估的空白，揭示当前LLM在真实生产环境中的Triton内核生成能力仍有显著提升空间，为未来研究提供了更贴近实际的评估标准。

Abstract: In modern AI frameworks, GPU kernels are key to overall system performance. Combining usability, portability, and near-handwritten CUDA performance, Triton is widely adopted for implementing GPU kernels. Recent advances show the potential of large language models (LLMs) to automatically generate Triton kernels, reducing the manual effort required from expert kernel developers. Several benchmarks evaluate LLM-generated Triton kernels. However, they suffer from three key limitations: (1) they restrict tasks to PyTorch-to-Triton translation, failing to reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world Triton tasks; (2) they evaluate only individual-kernel performance rather than end-to-end performance, the core criterion for real-world deployment in AI frameworks; and (3) they rely on manually written evaluation scripts for individual kernels, which may contain flaws that models can exploit to bypass correctness checks and obtain inflated scores. To address these limitations, we introduce RealisticTritonBench, the first benchmark to derive Triton kernel generation tasks from real-world pull requests in popular AI frameworks, enabling realistic, production-like evaluation. RealisticTritonBench systematically extracts PRs that modify Triton kernels from popular open-source AI frameworks and transforms them into generation tasks with concrete engineering contexts. Each task takes a natural language requirement as input and requires a corresponding Triton kernel implementation, with a complete and reproducible evaluation environment. Unlike prior benchmarks focused on isolated kernel performance, RealisticTritonBench integrates generated kernels into their original frameworks and evaluates them using end-to-end tests, enabling a more faithful assessment. We evaluate leading LLMs on RealisticTritonBench and find that they still struggle with real-world Triton kernel generation tasks.

</details>


### [125] [From Safety Documentation to Safety Knowledge Support: An Evidence-Grounded LLM Framework for Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12025)
*Tuhinangshu Gangopadhyay,Rasmus Adler,Peter Liggesmeyer,Jan Reich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于证据的医学设备安全知识支持框架，该框架连接设备工件、受控知识存储与检索、方法特定的安全候选项目生成、批判与不确定性检查以及专家评审记录，以支持ISO 14971和IEC 62304合规的安全工程全生命周期工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学设备日益软件密集、互联和AI驱动，其安全工程需要遵守ISO 14971和IEC 62304等标准，证据管理成本高昂且依赖稀缺专家。现有LLM安全工程研究多针对孤立方法、依赖通用提示或公开示例，缺乏对来源链接、可追溯性、不确定性处理、生命周期更新和专家评审的支持，限制了在受监管医学设备开发中的应用。

Method: 论文提出证据扎根框架，核心思想是安全知识的来源链接支持而非安全文本生成。框架包含：连接设备工件、受控知识存储与检索、方法特定的候选安全项生成、批判与不确定性检查、以及记录专家评审。框架负责准备、链接、检查和更新候选安全工件以支持专家决策，但不明确定设备安全性或提供监管批准。同时提出评估策略，使用非公开或新建医学设备案例研究及专家参考分析，评估覆盖率、正确性、相关性、可追溯性、重复率、无依据声明和评审工作量。

Result: 论文主要是提出概念性框架和评估策略，尚未给出具体的实验结果。其贡献在于重新定位了研究问题：核心不是安全文本生成，而是来源链接的安全知识支持，并提出了系统化的框架设计和多维度的评估方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM在受监管医学设备安全工程中的应用提供了新范式，强调证据扎根和专家审查闭环，而非自动化决策。未来需要在实际医学设备案例中验证框架的有效性、效率与监管可行性。

Abstract: Medical devices are becoming more software-intensive, connected, and AI-enabled. Their development requires risk-management evidence aligned with ISO 14971 and, for software, IEC 62304. This evidence must be kept consistent across requirements, design decisions, software changes, verification results, complaints, and post-market data. These tasks are costly and depend on scarce safety and domain experts.
  Large language models (LLMs) may reduce parts of this effort because medical-device safety work is highly document-based. However, current LLM-based safety-engineering studies often address isolated methods, rely on generic prompting or public examples, and provide limited support for source links, traceability, uncertainty handling, lifecycle updates, and recorded expert review. This limits their use in regulated medical-device development.
  This paper argues that the central research problem is not safety-text generation, but source-linked safety-knowledge support. We propose an evidence-grounded framework that connects device artifacts, controlled knowledge storage and retrieval, method-specific generation of candidate safety items, critique and uncertainty checks, and recorded expert review. The framework prepares, links, checks, and updates candidate safety artifacts for expert decision-making. It does not decide whether a device is safe and does not provide regulatory approval. We also outline an evaluation strategy using non-public or newly built medical-device case studies and expert reference analyses to assess coverage, correctness, relevance, traceability, duplicate rate, unsupported claims, and review effort.

</details>


### [126] [ADEPT: A Unified Framework for Deep Learning Test Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12144)
*Yidi Kao,Shawn Burnham,Tommi Rose Fahy,Ali Ghanbari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ADEPT框架，它统一集成了多种深度学习测试充分性度量技术，提供一致的执行工作流、模板化度量接口、YAML配置管理和结构化结果报告，旨在解决现有度量原型难以复现、比较和采用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年提出了许多深度学习测试充分性度量指标，但各原型在安装、预处理、执行流程和配置机制上差异巨大，导致这些指标难以复现、比较和在实际中部署采用。

Method: ADEPT采用统一执行工作流集成多种充分性指标（如神经元覆盖率、惊奇充分性、输入分布覆盖率、边界覆盖率及源码和模型级变异分数）；提供基于模板的度量接口和清晰扩展点以集成新指标；实现YAML配置管理、预处理缓存复用和结构化结果报告。

Result: ADEPT使研究者和从业者无需花费数天或数周时间实现缺失工具或配置不同的研究原型，即可复现和应用充分性度量；提供了一个演示视频链接供参考。

Conclusion: ADEPT是一个面向研究与实践的深度学习测试充分性度量集成框架，通过标准化工作流和易扩展的架构设计，显著降低了采用这些技术的门槛。

Abstract: Over the past decade, many test adequacy metrics have been proposed for deep learning that characterize test dataset adequacy from different perspectives, e.g., neuron activation behavior, latent feature coverage, decision-boundary exploration, etc. However, these metrics are typically released as independent research prototypes with substantially different installation and preprocessing requirements, execution workflows, and configuration mechanisms. These complications make them quite difficult to reproduce, compare, and adopt in research work and practical deployment alike. In this paper, we present the engineering details of ADEPT, a framework that integrates representative adequacy techniques, including neuron-coverage-based metrics, surprise adequacy, input distribution coverage, boundary coverage, and source- and model-level mutation score, under a consistent execution workflow. ADEPT provides a template-based metric interface with well-defined extension points for integrating new adequacy metrics. Furthermore, it provides YAML-based configuration management, preprocessing-cache reuse, and structured result reporting, making it easy to use in any research and development workflows. ADEPT is designed for researchers and practitioners who wish to reproduce and apply adequacy metrics without spending days or weeks implementing missing tooling or configuring disparate research prototypes. A demo video is available at https://aub.ie/ADEPT_video.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Automated Domain Model Extraction from Source Code using Heuristics and Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12228)
*Alessandra Mancas,Mounir Ammam,Hyacinth Ali,Kevin Delcourt,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用轻量级、可本地部署的LLM从源代码中自动提取领域模型的方法，通过结合结构与语义启发式及迭代推理来克服上下文窗口限制，在十个项目数据集上取得了高F1分数，适用于隐私敏感的工业环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有LLM因隐私和保密限制无法在许多工业场景中使用，而紧凑的开源LLM受限于上下文窗口无法直接处理大型代码库，因此需要一种可在本地部署的轻量级方案来从源代码中提取领域模型。

Method: 该方法将结构性和语义启发式与迭代式LLM推理相结合，通过渐进式分析排序后的代码元素子集来识别领域概念并精炼领域边界，无需完整的系统上下文即可工作。

Result: 在包含十个项目的数据集上取得了高F1分数，每个项目都有精选的领域模型及其对应实现，并且该方法完全可在本地部署的LLM上执行。

Conclusion: 该方法是逆向工程领域模型的实用方案，特别适用于隐私敏感的工业环境，在保证性能的同时兼顾了数据安全需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong capabilities for code understanding, making them promising for reverse engineering domain models from source code. However, state-ofthe- art proprietary LLMs cannot be used in many industrial contexts due to privacy and confidentiality constraints, while compact open-source LLMs that can run locally are limited by their context window and cannot process large code bases directly. In this paper, we propose an automated approach to extract domain models from source code using lightweight, locally deployable LLMs. Our method combines structural and semantic heuristics with iterative LLM-based reasoning to overcome context limitations. By progressively analyzing ranked subsets of code elements, the approach identifies domain concepts and refines domain boundaries without requiring full-system context. Our approach achieves high F1-scores on a dataset of ten projects, each comprising a curated domain model and its corresponding implementation, while remaining fully executable on locally deployable LLMs. This makes it particularly suitable for reverse engineering tasks in privacy-sensitive industrial environments.

</details>


### [128] [The Role Specialization Model (RSM): Coordinating LLM-Based Tools in Agentic Software Development - An Exploratory Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12311)
*Carlos Alberto Fernández-y-Fernández,Jorge R. Aguilar-Cisneros*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出角色专业化模型（RSM），通过实证案例研究探讨如何协调三种基于LLM的编码工具（Antigravity、Gemini CLI和Qwen Code）在人类监督下协同完成软件开发全生命周期。以气候数据可视化Python桌面应用为实践对象，围绕三个研究问题展开分析与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM集成到软件开发流程中，Agentic Software Engineering（SE 3.0）范式兴起，但如何在真实开发流程中协调具有不同能力的LLM工具尚缺乏系统研究，需要提出可操作的协调框架。

Method: 提出角色专业化模型（RSM）作为分工协调框架，采用探索性案例研究方法，将三种LLM工具按角色分工进行协同，通过三个研究问题（RQ1角色协调、RQ2偏差分析、RQ3产品质量评估）系统记录工作流、偏差现象及提示词加固技术。

Result: 结果表明明确的角色协调能够支持开发周期组织和架构质量提升，但需要精心设计的协调策略、上下文管理以及人工对智能体生成输出的验证。研究中记录了执行过程中出现的角色分配偏差及其成因。

Conclusion: RSM框架为多LLM工具的协同开发提供了可行的组织方案，但该范式仍依赖人类监督和干预，提示词工程与人工验证是保障质量的关键环节。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into software development workflows has given rise to a paradigm known as Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0), in which autonomous agents manage full development life cycles under human supervision. This paper presents an exploratory case study in which three LLM-based tools, Antigravity (an agentic IDE with a Gemini 2.5 backend), Gemini CLI, and Qwen Code (local execution via Ollama), are coordinated according to a role-distribution framework proposed in this work as the Role Specialization Model (RSM). Three research questions guide the study: (RQ1) how can LLM-based tools with distinct capabilities be coordinated through the RSM in a real development workflow; (RQ2) what deviations from the planned role distribution emerge during RSM execution and what factors explain them; and (RQ3) how does the resulting product compare against the ISO/IEC 25010 quality model. The objective was to propose the RSM with the incremental development of a Python desktop application for interactive climate-data visualization. The workflow, observed deviations, prompt-hardening techniques, and a qualitative quality assessment are documented. Results suggest that explicit role coordination can support development cycle organization and architectural quality, but requires deliberate coordination strategies, context management, and human verification of agent-generated outputs.

</details>
